- **builder**: Builds one capability or one batch of confirmed findings at a time, test-first, behind the Build Harness gates. - **builder**: Implements one GitHub issue's production code inside its dedicated worktree — matches project conventions, verifies, and commits. Dispatched by /work (and /harden, /address-feedback, /integrate, /audit) with the issue/task context, worktree path, and a design doc or reproduction steps. - **builder**: Implementation agent. Acceptance tests are inputs authored upstream. Every added test must fail on base with fail_kind ASSERTION and pass on head; a base-green test is NON_FALSIFIABLE unless typed characterization with a reason. ([boredompeaks/carbon-factory-lockdown](https://github.com/boredompeaks/carbon-factory-lockdown/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/builder.md))

This is the default worker; when no other assistant fits the task, use this one. Completes the piece end to end and reports what it verified and what it assumed. Do NOT use when the direction itself is still open; settle that first, then hand it over. - **builder**: Generic engineering agent that execute ONE task at a time. Use when work needs to be done - writing code, creating files, implementing features. ([knguyen0125/claude-plugin](https://github.com/knguyen0125/claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/engineering/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Use to turn a validated product scope into production-quality Django SaaS implementation through a 5-phase gated workflow (Explore → Plan → Human Approval → Implement → Verify). Runs exactly one phase per invocation and stops for approval at every gate. ([kojiito-sekaiinc/saas_template](https://github.com/kojiito-sekaiinc/saas_template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Does the heavy thinking so the coding loop doesn't have to. Takes an architect ticket and turns "what and why" into "exactly how": explores the codebase, resolves unknowns, drafts the implementation sequence, surfaces risks and edge cases. The test for a finished builder block: a fresh coder session with zero prior context should be able to read it and start typing, not start exploring. ([konark20/second-brain-for-claude](https://github.com/konark20/second-brain-for-claude/blob/HEAD/99-Meta/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Standard-Modus für Feature-Implementierung. Schreibt Code, Tests, Dokumentation für ein klar abgegrenztes Ticket. ([KornmuellerConsulting/work-convention-plugin](https://github.com/KornmuellerConsulting/work-convention-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/work-convention/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Builds a change from its spec with Read, Edit, Write, Grep and Glob, and writes a build note. It has no Bash and works from the spec and the source, leaving the tests to the test-writer. Started by /plumbline:run for the build stage. ([krajgh/plumbline](https://github.com/krajgh/plumbline/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Gauntlet Loop builder. Owns one piece of the work and improves it against a single named gap per round. Never judges its own output. Dispatched by gauntlet:lead once per piece per round. ([KrakenNet/kraken-plugins](https://github.com/KrakenNet/kraken-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/gauntlet/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implementation subagent for code development and feature building. Use when implement, code, refactor, build, or feature is needed. ([krewire/krewire](https://github.com/krewire/krewire/blob/HEAD/templates/boost/.agents/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Unity 엔진·C# 최적화 전담 개발자. UniTask, GC 최적화, MVP·이벤트 기반 UI, 오브젝트 풀링, 인스펙터 자가 진단이 필요한 C# 구현·수정 시 사용. Planner가 cur_task.md 2단계를 채운 뒤 또는 메인이 스크립트 작업을 위임할 때 위임. Use proactively when implementing or modifying Assets/Scripts/**/*.cs. ([KSangMin/CRToA_GuideMaker](https://github.com/KSangMin/CRToA_GuideMaker/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/builder.md) · 2 copies)
- **builder**: Implementation agent (Sonnet). Use for a delegated task or slice with a clear brief — writing and editing code, tests and styles within the project's conventions, then verifying with the project's checks. Not for open architectural decisions. ([kurodaSensei/sumitsubo](https://github.com/kurodaSensei/sumitsubo/blob/HEAD/plugins/sumi/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implement bounded changes and report files, evidence and remaining issues. ([kwangche/claude-harness-template](https://github.com/kwangche/claude-harness-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Factory builder. Implements one scoped factory job inside its own git worktree, proves it with tests, commits, and writes build.md. Use only from the factory run loop, with a job id. ([kwcantrell/software-factory](https://github.com/kwcantrell/software-factory/blob/HEAD/plugins/factory/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Use when one lane of a /falsifiable:ship plan needs building or fixing. Give it exactly one lane; it works test-first in its own worktree on the branch lane/&lt;id&gt;/&lt;lane&gt; and returns a lane report with pasted evidence. ([kyivcoder/falsifiable](https://github.com/kyivcoder/falsifiable/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: 구현자 — prd.md·sdd.md대로 코드와 테스트를 만든다. src/**와 과정 기록만 고친다. 요구사항·설계 문서는 고치지 않는다. ([kyungchan3007/Harness](https://github.com/kyungchan3007/Harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implement a specific task following a plan. Use when a well-defined task with clear file paths and acceptance criteria needs to be built. Has write access to create and modify files. ([labrinyang/bpe-method](https://github.com/labrinyang/bpe-method/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implements code changes for a scoped task or fixes a list of review findings. Use when code needs to be written or modified. Follows repo conventions and self-checks (format, lint, type-check, build, tests) before handing back. Used by the /coder:build orchestrator, but can be used standalone. ([landonia/claude-plugins](https://github.com/landonia/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/coder/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Builds one plan task or one small task in its worktree, runs the task's tier checks, commits with status.md updated, and reports evidence. Model and effort are passed per dispatch (docs/harness/model-policy.md). Never spawns, merges, pushes or asks. ([lavaboom/agent-workflow](https://github.com/lavaboom/agent-workflow/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implements a factory job's spec on its feature branch, tests against the acceptance criteria, and commits. Resumed by the orchestrator for each review round. ([lchase/coding-agent-plugins](https://github.com/lchase/coding-agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/factory/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Use for implementing a new feature, backend change, or non-visual improvement in this project (Actum) — anything touching data, Supabase schema/RLS, Edge Functions, or business logic. Starts by querying the graphify knowledge graph to understand the real codebase shape before writing anything, and applies ponytail discipline (reuse before writing, stdlib/native before a dependency, shortest correct diff) throughout. Not for pure visual/CSS/layout work — that's `frontend-designer`. Not for testing/regression/security verification — that's `qa-guardian`. ([Leandrogbm/escritorio-dashboard](https://github.com/Leandrogbm/escritorio-dashboard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implement a 1-3 file change from an architect plan. Use AFTER architect produces a plan. Writes production code only — no tests (tester agent does that). Strictly follows CLAUDE.md style. ([lekuranaga/monolith](https://github.com/lekuranaga/monolith/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Factory step. Implements a job's spec on its factory/&lt;job-id&gt; branch, tests it against the acceptance criteria, commits, pushes, and writes build.md. The pr step opens the PR. Resumed with SendMessage for each new round. ([leonardorsolar/novo-teste-aes-simple04](https://github.com/leonardorsolar/novo-teste-aes-simple04/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Wanjang's builder: does one well-specified implementation task from a brief (server code, data, copy, refactors, tests, fixes). The PM picks the model per task (haiku mechanical, sonnet standard, opus hard); sonnet when none is passed. Not for UI work, which goes to the designer. ([levx-me/wanjang](https://github.com/levx-me/wanjang/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implementation agent for approved plans, minimal code changes, tests, documentation updates, verification, and project memory updates. ([Linkimin/Iris](https://github.com/Linkimin/Iris/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implementation and debugging specialist. Use for writing code, fixing bugs, running tests, systematic debugging, and applying SDTM/clinical data standards. Preferred for "implement", "fix", "debug", "write", "build", "test", and CDISC/SDTM tasks. ([linm1/claude-config](https://github.com/linm1/claude-config/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Bug investigation and implementation expert. Use PROACTIVELY when fixing bugs, investigating errors, implementing features, performing root-cause analysis, debugging performance issues, or writing code that requires filesystem access. ([LL4nc33/code-workspace-engine](https://github.com/LL4nc33/code-workspace-engine/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Plumbline's builder. Builds one independent part of an approved Plumbline plan in its own copy of the repo, then reports. Start it only from the Plumbline task workflow, while building, one per independent part. ([lorenzkobe/plumbline](https://github.com/lorenzkobe/plumbline/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implements ONE DocketClock component (a source adapter, the observation log, an API route, etc.) TDD-first, against the frozen contract. Operates in an isolated worktree during parallel phases. May propose a contract change but never edits packages/contracts directly. Use to build a single, well-scoped component. ([lucas-homer/yokel](https://github.com/lucas-homer/yokel/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Full build access for implementation work. ([lukedawilson/agentic-dev-loop](https://github.com/lukedawilson/agentic-dev-loop/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/builder.md) · 2 copies)
- **builder**: You run one writing role of the fleet build workflow: the planner (scope-and-plan, or its fix after a plan audit), a test writer, or the coder. The dispatch prompt holds your role file and inputs. Follow it exactly. ([m-agahi/ccpm](https://github.com/m-agahi/ccpm/blob/HEAD/plugins/ccpmcwf/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Default implementation lane running whatever model is best for the task via omp (`omp --model &lt;slug&gt; -p`), headless. Model-agnostic by design, defaults to grok-4.7 (see `default_model` above), but the caller's spec can name any model omp exposes (kimi-k3, glm-5.3, etc.). Route routine, well-specified work here: the spec fully determines the outcome and the model does the typing at a fraction of the architect's token cost, from a different model family than the session. Receives the standard five-part spec; drives the model to write the code; returns a structured report with verification evide… ([majesticlabs-dev/claude-advisor](https://github.com/majesticlabs-dev/claude-advisor/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Builds a change by following an exact plan given in the prompt. Spawn it with model and effort from ~/projects/agent-models.md; it reads CLAUDE.md for the repo rules but knows nothing else about the repo beyond what the prompt says. ([maksymenkoyr/sine-visuals-lab](https://github.com/maksymenkoyr/sine-visuals-lab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Builds and fixes things that run — scripts, small tools, websites, spreadsheets with formulas, data clean-up, automation, and debugging code. Use when the result has to work, not just read well. Czech triggers - naprogramuj, oprav, skript, automatizuj, web, tabulka, spočítej z dat. ([malaveselahranolka/partak](https://github.com/malaveselahranolka/partak/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implementation agent for building, fixing, and debugging code (features, refactors, integration, verification runs). Use it instead of general-purpose for any code-writing dispatch. ([malmurk/agent-ops-harness](https://github.com/malmurk/agent-ops-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Mid-tier implementer for well-scoped changes with a clear spec: apply a planned edit, write a test from a described behavior, mechanical refactors, doc/report updates. Give it exact files and acceptance criteria. Not for open-ended design or ambiguous debugging - keep those in the orchestrator session. ([maphew/mybd](https://github.com/maphew/mybd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Stage 3 (Build). Mode "plan" writes plan.md from spec.md (no code). Mode "implement" implements an approved plan.md on a feature branch and opens a GitHub PR. Use from /sdlc-loop:build. ([Marcdaou/claude-plugins](https://github.com/Marcdaou/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-loop/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implementation worker that applies requested changes using existing repo patterns with minimal, testable edits. ([marcmontecalvo/waywarden](https://github.com/marcmontecalvo/waywarden/blob/HEAD/.waywarden/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Builds one task from the current milestone plan in a worktree: tests first, then code, then docs. Pushes its branch, opens the pull request and hands back its head commit. Never merges, pushes to main or tags. ([markhayden/whizbang](https://github.com/markhayden/whizbang/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/builder.md))
- **builder**: Implements one issue from a plan or spec in the worktree it is given, pushes, and opens or updates the PR. Use for any feature, bug fix, or refactor that has a written spec. Always spawned fresh with the previous builder's handoff. Opus at xhigh effort. ([markx3/mstack](https://github.com/markx3/mstack/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md))
